  Repeat.

Color identity: WB | Bracket: Spicy | Popularity: 0 | Commander: legal
Produces: Infinite creature LTB; Infinite creature ETB; Infinite creature sacrifice triggers; Infinite death triggers; Infinite surveil
Start: Mikaeus, the Unhallowed (battlefield); Bloodied Ghost (battlefield: without a +1/+1 counter on it); Umbral Collar Zealot (battlefield)
Steps: Activate Umbral Collar Zealot by sacrifiicing Bloodied Ghost.
  When Bloodied Ghost dies, its undying ability triggers, returning it from your graveyard to the battlefield with a +1/+1 counter on it. When it enters, Bloodied Ghost will put a -1/-1 counter on itself, removing the +1/+1 counter.
  Resolve the Umbral Collar Zealot ability.
  Repeat.

## Combo 63-1651-4013-6893
Cards: Wernog, Rider's Chaplain + Clock of Omens + Eldrazi Displacer + The Eternity Elevator
Color identity: WB | Bracket: Exhibition | Popularity: 0 | Commander: legal
Produces: Infinite artifact ETB; Infinite artifact LTB; Infinite artifact sacrifice triggers; Infinite artifact tokens; Infinite creature LTB; Infinite creature ETB; Infinite colored mana; Infinite colorless mana; Infinite card draw; Infinite draw triggers; Infinite blinking; Infinite untap of artifacts you control; Infinite Clue tokens; Infinite blinking of most creatures; Infinite Clue tokens for opponents
Start: Wernog, Rider's Chaplain (battlefield); Clock of Omens (battlefield); Eldrazi Displacer (battlefield); The Eternity Elevator (battlefield)
Prerequisites: The Eternity Elevator has twenty or more charge counters on it.
Steps: Activate The Eternity Elevator by tapping it, adding {C}{C}{C}.
  Activate Eldrazi Displacer by paying {2}{C}, blinking Wernog.
  When Wernog leaves and enters, it triggers twice, creating at least two Clue tokens.
  Activate Clock of Omens by tapping the Clues, untapping The Eternity Elevator.
  Repeat.
Notes: Because your opponents must choose to either let themselves and you investigate, or lose life, this combo will very likely result in infinite Clue tokens for you and your opponents.

Cards: Yawgmoth, Thran Physician + Dross Harvester + Field of Souls

Requires: Creature with Undying (battlefield)
Start: Yawgmoth, Thran Physician (battlefield); Dross Harvester (battlefield); Field of Souls (battlefield)

  Activate Yawgmoth by paying 1 life and sacrificing another creature.
  When the creature dies, Dross Harvester triggers, causing you to gain 2 life 
  Resolve the Yawgmoth ability, putting a -1/-1 counter on the undying creature and drawing a card.
  Repeat.

## Combo 643-1707-2818-4302
Cards: Last Laugh + Bishop of Wings + Maskwood Nexus + Floating Shield
Color identity: WB | Bracket: Exhibition | Popularity: 0 | Commander: legal
Produces: Infinite creature ETB; Infinite creature LTB; Infinite damage; Infinite death triggers; Infinite lifegain; Infinite lifegain triggers
Start: Last Laugh (battlefield); Bishop of Wings (battlefield); Maskwood Nexus (battlefield); Floating Shield (battlefield)
Prerequisites: Floating Shield attached to Bishop of Wings.
  "Black" chosen with Floating Shield.
  You control an additional creature with toughness equal to one.
  You have a way to deal damage to the creature.
  Your life total is at least 2.
Steps: Deal damage to a creature you control with toughness equal to one.
  The creature dies as a state-based action due to having zero toughness.
  The creature dies as an Angel due to Maskwood Nexus, triggering Bishop of Wings' second ability, causing you to create a 1/1 Spirit creature token.
  The Spirit enters the battlefield, as an Angel due to Maskwood Nexus, triggering Bishop of Wings' first ability, causing you to gain four life.
  Last Laugh triggers, dealing 1 damage to each creature except Bishop of WIngs, as well as each player.
  The Spirit dies as a state-based action due to having zero toughness.
  Repeat from step 3.

  Thornbite Staff attached to Fain.
  Fain does not have summoning sickness.

  Resolve the Fain trigger, untapping it.
  Resolve the Fain ability from step 1, putting two +1/+1 counters on a creature you control.
  Repeat.
